<script>


    $(document).ready(function () {

        overview = true;
        
        $("#macthResultsRecords div").eq(0).removeClass("container");

        $("#batting div").removeClass("container");
        $("#batting").removeClass("fade");

        $("#bowling div").removeClass("container");
        $("#bowling").removeClass("fade");

        $("#fielding div").removeClass("container");
        $("#fielding").removeClass("fade");

        $("#ranking div").removeClass("container");
        $("#ranking").removeClass("fade");


        
        $("div[name='sponsers']").css("border-bottom", "none");
        


        $("#errorMessage").dialog({
            autoOpen: false,
            modal: true
        });

        $('.table-responsive').on('shown.bs.dropdown', function (e) {
            var t = $(".about-table"),
                m = $(e.target).find('.dropdown-menu'),
                tb = t.offset().top + t.height(),
                mb = m.offset().top + m.outerHeight(true),
                d = 20; // Space for shadow + scrollbar.
            if (t[0].scrollWidth > t.innerWidth()) {
                if (mb + d > tb) {
                    t.css('padding-bottom', ((mb + d) - tb));
                }
            } else {
                t.css('overflow', 'visible');
            }
        }).on('hidden.bs.dropdown', function () {
            $(this).css({'padding-bottom': '', 'overflow': ''});
        });

        $("#dialog-confirm").dialog({
            resizable: false,
            autoOpen: false,
            modal: true,
            buttons: {
                "Delete": function () {
                    var teamId = $("#deleteId").val();
                    var ajaxUrl = '/TitansCricket/deleteTeam.do?clubId=32995&teamId=' + teamId;
                    $.ajax({
                        url: ajaxUrl,
                        success: function (result) {
                            if (result != '') {
                                $('#errorMessage').html(result);
                                $('#errorMessage').dialog("open");
                            } else {
                                $("#row" + teamId).remove();
                            }
                        }
                    });
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                },
                Cancel: function () {
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                }
            }
        });

        $("#dialog-confirm1").dialog({
            resizable: false,
            autoOpen: false,
            modal: true,
            buttons: {
                "Lock": function () {
                    var teamId = $("#lockId").val();
                    var ajaxUrl = '/TitansCricket/lockTeam.do?clubId=32995&teamId=' + teamId;
                    $.ajax({
                        url: ajaxUrl, success: function (result) {
                            if (result == 'success') {
                                $("#lockLink" + teamId).attr('href', "javascript:unLockTeam(" + teamId + ",\"" + $("#lockMessage").html() + "\");");
                                $("#lockLink" + teamId).html('<img alt="Unlock" title="Unlock" src="https://static.cricclubs.com/utilsv2/images/lock.png">');
                                // $(".linkAsButton").button();
                            } else {
                                $('#errorMessage').html("We could not process your request please contact support@cricclubs.com");
                                $('#errorMessage').dialog("open");
                            }

                        }
                    });
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                },
                Cancel: function () {
                    var matchId = $("#lockId").val();
                    // $("#lockButton"+matchId).button( "enable" );
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                }
            }
        });

        $("#dialog-confirm2").dialog({
            resizable: false,
            autoOpen: false,
            modal: true,
            buttons: {
                "UnLock": function () {
                    var teamId = $("#unLockId").val();
                    var ajaxUrl = '/TitansCricket/unLockTeam.do?clubId=32995&teamId=' + teamId;
                    $.ajax({
                        url: ajaxUrl, success: function (result) {

                            if (result == 'success') {
                                $("#lockLink" + teamId).attr("href", "javascript:lockTeam(" + teamId + ",\"" + $("#unLockMessage").html() + "\");");
                                $("#lockLink" + teamId).html('<img alt="Lock" title="Lock" src="https://static.cricclubs.com/utilsv2/images/unlock.png">');
                                //$(".linkAsButton").button();
                            } else {
                                $('#errorMessage').html("We could not process your request please contact support@cricclubs.com");
                                $('#errorMessage').dialog("open");
                            }

                        }
                    });
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                },
                Cancel: function () {
                    var matchId = $("#unLockId").val();
                    // $("#unLockButton"+matchId).button( "enable" );
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                }
            }
        });

        $("#dialog-confirm3").dialog({
            resizable: false,
            autoOpen: false,
            modal: true,

            buttons: {
                "Lock": function () {
                    var ajaxUrl = '/TitansCricket/lockAllTeams.do?clubId=32995&league=' + 949;
                    $.ajax({
                        url: ajaxUrl, success: function (result) {
                            if (result == 'success') {
                                window.location.href = window.location.href;
                            } else {
                                $('#errorMessage').html("We could not process your request please contact support@cricclubs.com");
                                $('#errorMessage').dialog("open");
                            }

                        }
                    });
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                },
                Cancel: function () {
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                }
            }
        });

        $("#dialog-confirm4").dialog({
            resizable: false,
            autoOpen: false,
            modal: true,
            buttons: {
                "UnLock": function () {
                    var ajaxUrl = '/TitansCricket/unlockAllTeams.do?clubId=32995&league=' + 949;
                    $.ajax({
                        url: ajaxUrl, success: function (result) {

                            if (result == 'success') {
                                window.location.href = window.location.href;
                            } else {
                                $('#errorMessage').html("We could not process your request please contact support@cricclubs.com");
                                $('#errorMessage').dialog("open");
                            }

                        }
                    });
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                },
                Cancel: function () {
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                }
            }
        });

    });

    function deleteTeam(teamId, name) {
        $("#deleteMessage").html(name);
        $("#deleteId").val(teamId);
        $("#dialog-confirm").dialog("open");
    }

    function lockTeam(teamId, name) {

        $("#lockMessage").html(name);
        $("#lockId").val(teamId);
        $("#dialog-confirm1").dialog("open");
    }

    function unLockTeam(teamId, name) {

        $("#unLockMessage").html(name);
        $("#unLockId").val(teamId);
        $("#dialog-confirm2").dialog("open");
    }

    function lockAllTeams() {
        $("#dialog-confirm3").dialog("open");
    }

    function unlockAllTeams() {
        $("#dialog-confirm4").dialog("open");
    }

    function refreshForTabs(view) {

        var currentURL = window.location.href;
        var queryString = '';
        if (currentURL.indexOf('?') != -1) {
            queryString = currentURL.substring(currentURL.indexOf('?'));
        } else {
            var selectedValue = $("#uplPLrSeriesdd").val().substring($("#uplPLrSeriesdd").val().indexOf('?'));
            queryString = selectedValue;
        }
        window.location.href = '/TitansCricket/' + "view" + view + '.do' + queryString;
    }

    function openRankingRecords() {
        window.location.href = '/TitansCricket/playerRankings.do?league=949&clubId=32995';
    }

    function openBowlingRecords() {
        window.location.href = '/TitansCricket/bowlingRecords.do?league=949&clubId=32995';
    }

    function openBattingRecords() {
        window.location.href = '/TitansCricket/battingRecords.do?league=949&clubId=32995';
    }

</script>

	$(function () {
	    $("#tablePlayerRankings").DataTable({"bPaginate": false, "bFilter": false, "bInfo": false });
	});
	
    
    var tableOffset = $("#tablePlayerRankings").offset().top;
    var $header = $("#tablePlayerRankings > thead").clone();
    var $fixedHeader = $("#header-fixed").append($header);

    $(window).bind("scroll", function() {
        var offset = $(this).scrollTop();
        
        if (offset >= tableOffset && $fixedHeader.is(":hidden")) {
            $fixedHeader.show();
        }
        else if (offset < tableOffset) {
            $fixedHeader.hide();
        }
        
        $("#header-fixed th").each(function(index){
            var index2 = index;
            $(this).width(function(index2){
                return $("#tablePlayerRankings th").eq(index).width();
            });
        });
        $("#header-fixed").width($("#tablePlayerRankings").width());
        
    });

    function rename(group) {
        $('#divGroup' + group).show();
    }

    function renameHide(group) {
        $('#divGroup' + group).hide();
    }

    function saveName(group) {
        var newName = $('#newName' + group).val();

        var ajaxUrl = '/TitansCricket/renameGroupName.do?clubId=32995&leagueId=949&groupId=' + group + '&groupName=' + newName;
        $.ajax({
            url: ajaxUrl, success: function (result) {

                if (result == 'success') {
                    $('#groupName' + group).html(newName);
                } else {
                    $('#errorMessage').html("We could not process your request please contact support@cricclubs.com");
                    $('#errorMessage').dialog("open");
                }

            }
        });

        $('#divGroup' + group).hide();
    }

<script type="text/javascript">
    $(document).ready(function () {

/////// Mobile Install app Ends
        var linkOfApp = "https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.cricclubs&hl=en_US";
        $("#open-app-confirm").dialog({
            resizable: false,
            autoOpen: false,
            modal: true,
            buttons: {
                "Install": function () {
                    window.location = linkOfApp;
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                },
                "Never Ask": function () {
                    localStorage.setItem("dontAskMobileApp", true);
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                },
                Cancel: function () {
                    $(this).dialog("close");
                }
            }
        });

        function openInApp(appStoreName) {
            $("#shoqAppStoreName").html(appStoreName);
            $("#open-app-confirm").dialog("open");
        }

        function checkAndOpenForMobileUser() {
            var userAgent = navigator.userAgent || navigator.vendor || window.opera;

            if (userAgent.match(/iPad/i) || userAgent.match(/iPhone/i) || userAgent.match(/iPod/i)) {
                linkOfApp = "https://apps.apple.com/us/app/cricclubs/id978682715"
                openInApp('App Store');

            } else if (userAgent.match(/Android/i)) {
                linkOfApp = 'https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.cricclubs&hl=en_US;';
                openInApp('Play Store');
            }
        }

        
/////// Mobile Install app Ends

        $("#dialog-confirm-TnC").dialog({
            resizable: false,
            autoOpen: false,
            modal: true,
            width: '80%'
        });


        var isMobile = {
            Android: function () {
                return navigator.userAgent.match(/Android/i);
            },
            BlackBerry: function () {
                return navigator.userAgent.match(/BlackBerry/i);
            },
            iOS: function () {
                return navigator.userAgent.match(/iPhone|iPad|iPod/i);
            },
            Opera: function () {
                return navigator.userAgent.match(/Opera Mini/i);
            },
            Windows: function () {
                return navigator.userAgent.match(/IEMobile/i);
            },
            any: function () {
                return (isMobile.Android() || isMobile.BlackBerry() || isMobile.iOS() || isMobile.Opera() || isMobile.Windows());
            }
        };
        $(".calendarBox").datepicker();

        

        $(document).on("click", '.whatsapp', function () {
            if (isMobile.any()) {
                var text = document.title;
                var url = window.location.href;
                var message = encodeURIComponent(text) + " - " + encodeURIComponent(url);
                var whatsapp_url = "whatsapp://send?text=" + message;
                window.location.href = whatsapp_url;
            } else {
                alert("Please share this article in mobile device");
            }
        });
        
    });

    $(document).ready(function() {


        $("#htbCloseButton").click(function () {
            $(".homeTopBanner").css("display", "none");
            sessionStorage.setItem("htbclose",true);
            $("#headerNavIcon").css("top", " -65px");
        });
        if(sessionStorage.getItem("htbclose")===null){

            $(".homeTopBanner").css("display", "block");
            $("#headerNavIcon").css("top", " -30px");
        }
        else{
            $(".homeTopBanner").css("display", "none");
            $("#headerNavIcon").css("top", " -65px");
        }

    });
